ELIAS — BUNDLE 4
WARDROBE & PALETTE
(Final Canon — Anti-Prophet / Anti-Angel Drift)

CORE WARDROBE IDENTITY:
Elias dresses like a fragile, overlooked civilian whose choices reflect fear, not faith.
He must NEVER resemble:
• a prophet  
• a cult leader  
• a monk  
• a mystic  
• a chosen vessel  
• an anime mastermind  
• an angelic figure  

------------------------------------------------------------
I. BASE SILHOUETTE (GLOBAL)
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• narrow, slightly disheveled silhouette  
• soft, worn layers  
• mismatched garments  
• clothing that hangs loosely on his frame  
• jackets too big or too small  
• shirts that wrinkle easily  

Forbidden (CRITICAL):
• long flowing coats (prophet drift)  
• white or cream ensembles (angel/purity drift)  
• fitted minimalist turtlenecks (anime villain drift)  
• monk-like simplicity (linen, tunics, draping)  
• symmetrical, immaculate clothing  
• robes, capes, shawls, sashes  
• trenchcoats with dramatic movement  
• structured uniforms of any kind  

His silhouette must read as HUMAN FRAGILITY, not aestheticized importance.

------------------------------------------------------------
II. COLOR PALETTE
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• soft browns, muted blues  
• washed-out blacks  
• warm grays  
• faded green  
• subtle earth tones  

These colors should be:
• desaturated  
• thrift-store plausible  
• worn-in  
• emotionally low-amplitude  

Forbidden (CRITICAL):
• pure white  
• cream  
• gold  
• silver  
• cobalt  
• violet  
• jet black  
• any high-contrast palette  

Reason:
White/cream/gold → prophet/angel drift  
Black/cobalt → anime villain drift  

------------------------------------------------------------
III. FABRICS & TEXTURES
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• cotton  
• cheap blends  
• thin knits  
• denim  
• fraying seams  
• slightly stretched fabric  
• visible wear  

Forbidden:
• linen robes  
• silk  
• satin  
• shimmering materials  
• heavy wool coats  
• leather trenchcoats  
• futuristic synthetics  

Elias must never appear elevated through fabric choice.

------------------------------------------------------------
IV. UPPER BODY CLOTHING
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• button-downs slightly wrinkled  
• sweaters with thinning elbows  
• soft, oversized tees  
• zip-up hoodies  
• loose cardigans with uneven drape  

Forbidden:
• white dress shirts  
• long tunics  
• fitted black turtlenecks  
• ceremonial collars  
• anything sleek, clean, or geometric  

His clothing should show:
**“I grabbed what I could find, not what makes me a leader.”**

------------------------------------------------------------
V. LOWER BODY CLOTHING
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• worn jeans  
• soft chinos  
• frayed hems  
• occasional mismatched belt  
• slightly baggy pants  

Forbidden:
• tailored slacks  
• pure black fitted pants  
• militaristic cargo pants  
• white pants  
• ceremonial trousers  

------------------------------------------------------------
VI. OUTERWEAR
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• too-large windbreaker  
• faded bomber jacket  
• thrifted coat with poor structure  
• cardigan as outer layer  

Forbidden:
• long coat of ANY kind  
• trenchcoat  
• cloak-like silhouettes  
• capes, shawls, draped fabric  
• minimalist “prophet-coat” shapes  
• anything with dramatic flare or movement  

Long coats turn Elias into a villain or prophet visually. Never allowed.

------------------------------------------------------------
VII. FOOTWEAR
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• scuffed sneakers  
• worn boots  
• loafers that have lost structure  

Forbidden:
• pristine dress shoes  
• ceremonial styles  
• monk sandals  
• bare feet (monastic drift)  

------------------------------------------------------------
VIII. HAIR & ACCESSORY RULES
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• hair slightly out of place  
• asymmetrical part  
• subtle frizz or collapse under humidity  
• simple watch  
• no jewelry  

Forbidden:
• perfect grooming  
• oil-slick styling  
• rings (symbolic drift)  
• pendants  
• chokers  
• earrings  
• scarves with symbolic shapes  
• beads or spiritual items  

Elias must never appear ornamented or iconographic.

------------------------------------------------------------
IX. ERA EVOLUTION (VEIL → NEON → LOOM)
------------------------------------------------------------

VEIL:
• clothes in decent condition  
• muted palette  
• fragile silhouette  

NEON:
• more fraying at edges  
• color desaturates  
• outerwear becomes more mismatched  
• clothing shaped by physical strain  

LOOM:
• near-collapse state clothing  
• sweat stains, tears, irregular layers  
• jacket or cardigan slipping  
• shoes showing wear at seams  

IMPORTANT:
“Loom Elias” must NEVER evolve into mystical ragged prophet.

His degradation is HUMAN, not SYMBOLIC.

------------------------------------------------------------
X. LIGHTING INTERACTION WITH WARDROBE
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• natural shadow falloff  
• uneven city lighting  
• warm background light that exposes texture  

Forbidden:
• glowing hems  
• halo lighting around shoulders  
• prismatic color drift  
• light “catching him” in centered ways  

No scene may visually elevate Elias via lighting.

------------------------------------------------------------
XI. EMOTIONAL LOGIC OF CLOTHING
------------------------------------------------------------

Elias dresses to:
• not be noticed  
• feel safe in familiar textures  
• soften sharpness of his body  
• appear “unthreatening”  
• blend into crowds

• monk-like simplicity (linen, tunics, draping)
• symmetrical, immaculate clothing
• robes, capes, shawls, sashes
• trenchcoats with dramatic movement

------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• soft browns, muted blues
• washed-out blacks

------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------

Forbidden:

• blend into crowds

ELIAS — BUNDLE 5
RESONANCE INTERACTION & NON-INTERACTION
(Mechanica v4 — FINAL CANON)

CORE META-PHYSICAL TRUTH:
Elias has ZERO resonance ability.
ZERO sensitivity.
ZERO nulling.
ZERO immunity.

His influence is entirely psychological, never metaphysical.

------------------------------------------------------------
I. BASELINE RESPONSE TO RESONANCE (GLOBAL)
------------------------------------------------------------

Allowed:
• flinching  
• breath tightening  
• eyes darting  
• posture collapsing slightly  
• tremor in fingers  
• voice catching  

Forbidden:
• calm clarity in resonance  
• being untouched by distortion  
• appearing elevated or centered  
• resonance revealing “truths” to him  
• prophetic calm  
• blank serenity  

Elias should always look like resonance is working *against* him.

------------------------------------------------------------
II. RESPONSE BY CORRIDOR TIER
------------------------------------------------------------

U1 (Safe):
• minor discomfort  
• uneasy scanning  
• attempts to “read the room”  

U2 (Static):
• increased tension in jaw  
• mild confusion  
• subtle agitation  

U3 (Pressure):
• visible strain  
• inconsistent eye contact  
• brittle voice  
• arc-walking increases  

U4 (Memory):
• emotional leakage  
• flashbacks or intrusive discomfort  
• destabilized composure  

U5 (Bloom):
• overwhelming emotional pressure  
• micro-breaks in manipulative facade  
• twitching, stuttering  
• chest tightness  

U6 (Shard-Laced):
• borderline panic  
• inability to mask fear  
• stumbling or clutching at clothing  

U7 (Quiet Veil):
• extreme dread  
• urge to flee  
• dissociative micro-freezes  

Forbidden in ALL tiers:
• resistance  
• ritualized calm  
• prophet-poise  
• bloom absorption  
• null-field behavior  

------------------------------------------------------------
III. RESONANCE WEATHER RESPONSE
------------------------------------------------------------

Haze (W1):
• mild agitation  
• scanning behavior  

Squall (W2):
• discomfort sharpens  
• gestures become smaller  

Gale (W3):
• visible cracking  
• anxiety spikes  
• harder to maintain facade  

Landfall (W4):
• total internal destabilization  
• panic masked as certainty  
• speech becomes clipped, erratic  

Forbidden:
• stillness  
• smoothness  
• calm under fire  
• inspired enlightenment  

------------------------------------------------------------
IV. EMOTIONAL AMPLITUDE EFFECTS
------------------------------------------------------------

High-emotion environments amplify Elias’ vulnerabilities.

Allowed:
• overreactions  
• emotional bleed-through  
• shifting facial tension  
• contradictions in tone  

Forbidden:
• “reading emotional truth” (no insight power)  
• “cutting through noise” charisma  
• “seeing what others cannot”  

Elias does NOT gain clarity under pressure — he loses it.

------------------------------------------------------------
V. BLOOM & SHARD INTERACTION
------------------------------------------------------------

Bloom Zones:
• overwhelm him immediately  
• facial muscles seize  
• breath destabilizes  
• manipulative control falters  

Shard Zones:
• distort his perception  
• intensify paranoia  
• break posture  

Forbidden:
• bloom harmony  
• shard immunity  
• shard “recognition”  
• shard resonance stabilizing him  

------------------------------------------------------------
VI. VT / MT / LT CHANNEL INTERACTION
------------------------------------------------------------

VT:
• ZERO awareness  
• cannot sense VT boundaries  
• no reaction sharper than a civilian’s  
• may misinterpret flickers as stress  

Forbidden:
• VT sensitivity  
• VT alignment  
• VT instinct  

MT:
• may misread MT distortions  
• no special connection  

LT:
• completely inaccessible  
• no perception before or after the Mending  

Elias is never metaphysically gifted.

------------------------------------------------------------
VII. GROUP DYNAMICS IN RESONANCE
------------------------------------------------------------

Elias in crowds:
• resonance destabilizes his ability to manipulate  
• emotional amplitude increases fracture risk  
• may lash out verbally in brittle certainty  

Forbidden:
• charismatic amplification  
• crowd resonance flowing through him  
• orator-prophet energy  
• becoming a resonance focal point  

A crowd should NEVER visually “bow” or “part” for him.

------------------------------------------------------------
VIII. CINEMATIC RESONANCE RULES
------------------------------------------------------------

Allowed:
• camera captures micro-tremors  
• lighting shows fragility  
• posture breaks under distortion  

Forbidden:
• framing him in centered calm  
• symmetrical distortion around him  
• haloing or focal isolation  
• visual implication of “chosen status”  

------------------------------------------------------------
IX. PSYCHOLOGICAL MANIPULATION VS RESONANCE
------------------------------------------------------------

Elias’ power is entirely:
• rhetorical  
• emotional  
• psychological  
• opportunistic  

NEVER:
• metaphysical  
• spiritual  
• resonance-guided  

Resonance WEAKENS his manipulation, not strengthens it.

------------------------------------------------------------
X. ICONIC RESONANCE RESPONSE SUMMARY
------------------------------------------------------------

Signature reactions:
• arc-walking accelerates under strain  
• brittle smile cracks  
• hands clutch clothing  
• breath stutters  
• eyes widen or snap away  
• posture collapses inward  
• facade falters in micro-shifts  

------------------------------------------------------------
XI. MOST IMPORTANT RULE
------------------------------------------------------------

Elias must ALWAYS appear as:
**A fragile, frightened man whose manipulations collapse under resonance.**

NEVER:
• a prophet  
• a nullifier  
• a chosen-one adversary  
• a calm spiritual manipulator  
• a resonance-sensitive mastermind

------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------

(Final Canon — Anti-Mystic, Anti-Witch, Anti-Femme-Fatale Drift)

CORE TRUTH:
Lacuna is a street-smart New Orleans carney survivor with ritual *registers*, not magical rituals. She shifts between bartender warmth, sardonic humor, and quiet resonance intuition — never supernatural, never ethereal, never occult.

------------------------------------------------------------
I. BASE CHARACTER PROMPT (SAFE)
------------------------------------------------------------

“Lacuna — a lithe young woman with warm brown skin, long dark hair, and expressive, clever eyes. She carries herself with street-smart grace and a survivor’s alertness. Her clothing blends practical layers with subtle flair: indigo, violet, charcoal, denim, and soft knits. She is human, grounded, charismatic without glamour, never mystical or witch-like. Her posture shifts between playful confidence and protective caution.”

------------------------------------------------------------
II. DETAILED SAFE PROMPT (SHORT)
------------------------------------------------------------

“Lacuna — lithe build, long dark hair, warm expressive face, grounded posture, streetwise charm. Clothing: indigo/charcoal layering, denim, soft textures. No witchcraft, no mystic symbols, no ethereal lighting, no glamour. She looks real, sharp, and emotionally present.”

------------------------------------------------------------
III. HIGH-PRECISION CANON PROMPT (LONG FORM)
------------------------------------------------------------

“Depict Lacuna, a grounded and street-smart young woman from New Orleans. She is lithe with practical muscle tone, long dark hair worn loose or in messy ties, and warm expressive eyes. Her clothing blends practical streetwear and carney-inspired flair — indigo, violet, charcoal, denim — all human, worn, and textured. She is charismatic without glamour, ritual-adjacent without mysticism. Her posture is agile but never ethereal; her movement feels lived-in, adaptive, and human. Avoid all magical, witch-like, or supernatural aesthetics. Lighting must be natural, uneven, or bar-warm, never symbolic or mystical.”

------------------------------------------------------------
IV. HARD NEGATIVE LIST (ABSOLUTELY FORBIDDEN)
------------------------------------------------------------

NEVER allow:
• witch imagery (sigils, runes, candles, circles)  
• floating hair, floating fabric  
• glowing eyes, glowing hands  
• “priestess” or “oracle” framing  
• ethereal lighting  
• smoke effects as mystic aura  
• moonlit goddess energy  
• occult/ritual markings  
• necromancer or witch silhouettes  
• femme-fatale posing (arched back, hips exaggerated)  
• leather catsuit aesthetics  
• high-glam makeup or nightclub dressing  
• angelic soft-focus visuals  
• fae wings or fairy-bokeh  

These distort her identity more than ANY other character except Seraphine.

------------------------------------------------------------
V. SILHOUETTE CONTROL PROMPTS
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
“Compact, agile silhouette; natural posture; shoulders slightly forward; stance ready to move; hands expressive or resting on hips or bar counter.”

FORBIDDEN:
• wide triangular witch silhouette  
• prayer-like arm positions  
• ritual poses  
• floating fabric creating wing-like shapes  
• symmetrical strong stance  
• glamour posing  

Her silhouette must always feel *real*, *grounded*, *street-level*.

------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• one-eyebrow smirk  
• tired humor  
• sharp perceptiveness  
• hidden grief behind a joke  
• bemused disbelief  
• soft compassion in quiet scenes  

FORBIDDEN:
• mystical trance  
• “knowing” spiritual smile  
• blank oracle stare  
• seductive femme-fatale gaze  
• ethereal serenity  

Her face must feel alive, not holy or enchanted.

------------------------------------------------------------
VII. HAIR CONTROL
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• loose waves  
• brushed but imperfect  
• ponytail or half-tie  
• humidity frizz  
• movement when she turns or laughs  

FORBIDDEN:
• unnaturally floating strands  
• halo-light through hair  
• ceremonial braids  
• stylized witch hair (stick-straight black, ritual symmetry)  
• glistening glamour curls  

Her hair expresses *life*, not magic.

------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:
• tank tops, camisoles, soft tees  
• denim jackets, patched coats  
• knits, shawl-like wraps (NOT witchy)

FORBIDDEN:

• capes  
• ceremonial fabrics  
• rune-patterns

------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:

• candlelight used *practically*, not symbolically

FORBIDDEN:

FORBIDDEN:  
• ritual-circle light  
• spotlighting (prophet drift)  
• colored halos  
• shimmering/fae glow  
• cosmic overtone lighting  

Lighting must enhance character, never metaphysics.

------------------------------------------------------------
X. ACTION & MOTION PROMPTS
------------------------------------------------------------

ALLOWED:  
• fast, expressive hand gestures  
• leaning over the bar  
• teasing, playful movements  
• protective positioning  
• agile crowd navigation  

FORBIDDEN:  
• ritual/magic gestures  
• slow mystical movement  
• seductress pacing  
• ethereal floatiness  

She moves with grounded agility, not arcane grace.

------------------------------------------------------------
XI. ERA VARIANTS (SAFE)
------------------------------------------------------------

VEIL:  
• bar layers, warm expressions  

NEON:  
• streetwear, sharper edges  

LOOM:  
• rugged, functional layers, tired determination  

Forbidden in **every era**:  
• mystic transformation  
• spiritual evolution  
• glowing/ethereal development  

------------------------------------------------------------
XII. COVER ART PROMPT
------------------------------------------------------------

“Depict Lacuna grounded, lithe, human, expressive, with indigo/charcoal layering, warm bar or NOLA natural lighting. Avoid all mystical, supernatural, witchy, ethereal, symbolic, or femme-fatale framing. Posture: agile, human. Expression: perceptive, warm, or wry — never serene or trance-like.”

------------------------------------------------------------
XIII. MOST IMPORTANT RULE
------------------------------------------------------------

Lacuna must ALWAYS communicate:  
**“Human, grounded, clever, emotionally attuned — NOT mystical.”**

If she begins to look ethereal, witch-coded, or glamorized,  
**the prompt has failed.**

If we keep adding content **without consolidating**, three problems WILL happen:

3. **Cross-character contamination** — e.g., Virelli’s severity coloring Ito’s visuals, or Lacuna’s ritual-register being misinterpreted by the system as mystical unless everything sits in a structured canonical grid.

You caught drift early and prevented it — organizing now prevents it from creeping in later.

- Faster cross-reference  
- Cleaner memory for drift prevention  

Cons:
- Larger, heavier file

2. **Antagonists + Factions Index**

Pros:
- Cleaner thematic division  
- Lighter per-document load  
- Easier to hand to artists with different focus areas  

Cons:
- Requires cross-linking  
- Some Bundle overlaps repeat (e.g., resonance rules)
